## Summary
<h2>2026.8.2</h2>
<h3>Highlights</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>Your Home agent, beside your work:</strong> open Home in a right or bottom dock with <code>Cmd/Ctrl+Shift+H</code>, keep your current page in view, and preview or remove its work-context snapshot or attach selected text to your message. Related <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5295045757" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/133632" data-hovercard-type="issue" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/issues/133632/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/133632">#133632</a>. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5295333543" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/133676" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/133676/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/133676">#133676</a>)</li>
<li><strong>A desktop companion for Linux:</strong> install the <code>.deb</code> or AppImage on x86-64 Linux, connect to a local or remote Gateway, and open Quick Chat from the system tray or an X11 keyboard shortcut. AppImage updates are signature-verified; <code>.deb</code> installs remain under your package manager. See the <a href="https://docs.openclaw.ai/platforms/linux" rel="nofollow">Linux guide</a>.</li>
<li><strong>Start work without switching pages:</strong> create and run a background session from New Session, keep its selected local, cloud, or paired-device placement, and open it from the completion notice. Related <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5224010434" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/128037" data-hovercard-type="issue" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/issues/128037/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/128037">#128037</a>. <h3>Changes</h3>
<ul>
<li>
<p><strong>Recovery cleanup:</strong> preview retained migration originals with <code>openclaw update cleanup --dry-run</code>, then explicitly remove eligible originals while the selected Gateway is stopped; cleanup preserves current SQLite history but permanently gives up rollback to removed originals. Related <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5296598709" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/133805" data-hovercard-type="issue" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/issues/133805/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/133805">#133805</a>. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5297107577" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/133864" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/133864/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/133864">#133864</a>)</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><strong>Session visibility default:</strong> let unsandboxed sessions work with other sessions of the same agent by default, including retained cron sessions; shared-agent operators should set <code>tools.sessions.visibility</code> to <code>tree</code> or <code>self</code> when they need narrower access, while sandbox and cross-agent restrictions remain enforced. Related <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5293746946" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/133456" data-hovercard-type="issue" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/issues/133456/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/133456">#133456</a>. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5293883862" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/133469" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/133469/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/133469">#133469</a>)</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><strong>Cross-session conversations:</strong> render forwarded messages as distinct speech bubbles with source-session links and sending-agent identity, preserving attribution when messages steer an active conversation. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5281628498" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/132054" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/132054/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/132054">#132054</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5293619150" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/133439" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/133439/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/133439">#133439</a>)</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><strong>Session organization:</strong> group session actions into clearer menus, copy transcripts as Markdown, open sessions in tabs, windows, or splits, edit icons and colors together, and optionally hide empty session groups. Related <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5294008675" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/133480" data-hovercard-type="issue" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/issues/133480/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/133480">#133480</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5295036913" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/133629" data-hovercard-type="issue" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/issues/133629/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/133629">#133629</a>. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5294088178" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/133490" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/133490/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/133490">#133490</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5295055313" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/133638" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/133638/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/133638">#133638</a>)</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><strong>Home without switching pages:</strong> dock the selected agent’s existing Home conversation beside your work, retain its draft and attachments when opening it full page, and inspect or remove the “Working on” context before sending. Related <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5295045757" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/133632" data-hovercard-type="issue" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/issues/133632/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/133632">#133632</a>. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5295333543" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/133676" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/133676/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/133676">#133676</a>)</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><strong>Prepared cloud projects:</strong> reuse prepared project snapshots and validated workspace hashes before starting a cloud session, and preserve the required stop, snapshot, and restart cycle for Daytona-backed projects. Related <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5293593120" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/133436" data-hovercard-type="issue" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/issues/133436/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/133436">#133436</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5293686845" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/133450" data-hovercard-type="issue" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/issues/133450/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/133450">#133450</a>. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5293661873" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/133447" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/133447/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/133447">#133447</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5298890662" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/134026" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/134026/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/134026">#134026</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5299087478" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/134043" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/134043/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/134043">#134043</a>)</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><strong>Readable Beam links:</strong> share transcripts through readable <code>/beam/</code> URLs named after their sessions, with existing access checks and contextual navigation preserved. Related <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5180343604" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/125752" data-hovercard-type="issue" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/issues/125752/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/125752">#125752</a>. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5180394255" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/125755" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/125755/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/125755">#125755</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5293798291" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/133463" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/133463/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/133463">#133463</a>)</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><strong>Chrome relay wake-up:</strong> support standalone local relays that start on demand, share their paired browser with the Gateway, and remain available to other CDP clients when the Gateway disconnects; see the <a href="https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/chrome-extension#standalone-direct-loopback-relay" rel="nofollow">Chrome extension guide</a> for supported builds and setup. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5229226742" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/128379" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/128379/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/128379">#128379</a>)</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><strong>Plugin SDK types:</strong> type Telegram <code>botToken</code> as <code>SecretInput</code> (<code>string | SecretRef</code>), matching existing secret-reference support; plugin authors should use the resolved account <code>token</code> and read finalization-context <code>messages</code> only when <code>source === "openclaw-transcript"</code>. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5298371518" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/133988" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/133988/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/133988">#133988</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5302121185" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/134238" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/134238/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/134238">#134238</a>)</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><strong>Background sessions:</strong> start work from New Session without leaving the page with <code>Cmd/Ctrl+Enter</code>, retain the selected local, cloud, or paired-device placement, and open the session from its completion notice; use <code>Cmd/Ctrl+Shift+Enter</code> when Modifier+Enter is already your normal send shortcut, while explicit Draft visibility still creates a draft. Related <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5224010434" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/128037" data-hovercard-type="issue" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/issues/128037/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/128037">#128037</a>. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5224249414" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/128050" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/128050/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/128050">#128050</a>) Thanks <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/Takhoffman/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/Takhoffman">@Takhoffman</a>.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><strong>iOS composer:</strong> bring inline model, thinking, permission, attachment, and context controls closer to the web experience, keeping queued sends bound to the session settings that authorized them. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5287440967" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/132683" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/132683/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/132683">#132683</a>) Thanks <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/Solvely-Colin/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/Solvely-Colin">@Solvely-Colin</a>.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><strong>Plugin approval verification:</strong> let plugins describe an external verification choice in approval presentations while OpenClaw retains approval identity, authorization, timeouts, and the final decision. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="4974410865" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/113517" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/113517/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/113517">#113517</a>) Thanks <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/Guardiola31337/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/Guardiola31337">@Guardiola31337</a>.</p>
</li>
</ul>
<h3>Fixes</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>Optional image decoding:</strong> update the release’s managed Sharp dependency to 0.35.4 with libheif 1.23.2, fixing vulnerabilities in image decoding while preserving optional installation. See the plugin dependency caveat under Known issues.</li>
<li><strong>Workspace permissions:</strong> apply permission changes to active runs and preserve session tool policies on cloud workers, so changing where work runs does not widen what it may do. <h3>Known issues</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>Optional memory plugin dependency:</strong> standalone <code>@openclaw/memory-lancedb</code> installs can still resolve an older vulnerable Sharp version through an optional Transformers dependency. The plugin’s reviewed text-embedding and vector-storage path does not load that image adapter; this dependency exposure remains a packaging follow-up.</li>
<li><strong>Hindi and Korean token labels:</strong> the native app’s token-usage label reverses the wording for used and total tokens; the counts and calculations are unchanged.</li>
</ul>
<h3>Upcoming deprecations</h3>
<p>These already-deprecated Plugin SDK import paths remain available in <strong>2026.8.2</strong>. Their recorded removal target is <strong>2026-09-01</strong>; plugin authors should prepare the following migrations using the <a href="https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/sdk-migration" rel="nofollow">Plugin SDK migration guide</a>.</p>
<ul>
<li><strong><code>openclaw/plugin-sdk/config-runtime</code>:</strong> use <code>api.pluginConfig</code> for plugin configuration, <code>openclaw/plugin-sdk/config-contracts</code> for types, <code>openclaw/plugin-sdk/runtime-config-snapshot</code> for snapshot reads, and <code>openclaw/plugin-sdk/config-mutation</code> for writes. (<code>plugin-sdk-config-runtime-subpath</code>)</li>
<li><strong><code>openclaw/plugin-sdk/channel-reply-pipeline</code>:</strong> import reply pipeline helpers from <code>openclaw/plugin-sdk/channel-outbound</code>. (<code>plugin-sdk-channel-reply-pipeline-subpath</code>)</li>
<li><strong><code>openclaw/plugin-sdk/infra-runtime</code>:</strong> move helpers to focused imports, including <code>openclaw/plugin-sdk/delivery-queue-runtime</code>, <code>openclaw/plugin-sdk/diagnostic-runtime</code>, <code>openclaw/plugin-sdk/error-runtime</code>, <code>openclaw/plugin-sdk/exec-approvals-runtime</code>, <code>openclaw/plugin-sdk/fetch-runtime</code>, and <code>openclaw/plugin-sdk/ssrf-runtime</code>. The migration guide records that system-event snapshot inspection and consumption still have no modern public replacement. (<code>plugin-sdk-infra-runtime-subpath</code>)</li>
<li><strong><code>openclaw/plugin-sdk/channel-lifecycle</code>:</strong> use <code>openclaw/plugin-sdk/channel-outbound</code>. (<code>plugin-sdk-channel-lifecycle-subpath</code>)</li>
<li><strong><code>openclaw/plugin-sdk/channel-message</code>:</strong> use <code>openclaw/plugin-sdk/channel-outbound</code> and <code>openclaw/plugin-sdk/channel-inbound</code>. (<code>plugin-sdk-channel-message-subpath</code>)</li>
</ul>

## Why It Matters
Source signal: <h2>2026.8.2</h2>
<h3>Highlights</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>Your Home agent, beside your work:</strong> open Home in a right or bottom dock with <code>Cmd/Ctrl+Shift+H</code>, keep your current page in view, and preview or remove its work-context snapshot or attach selected text to your message. Related <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5295045757" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/133632" data-hovercard-type="issue" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/issues/133632/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/133632">#133632</a>. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5295333543" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/133676" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/133676/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/133676">#133676</a>)</li>
<li><strong>A desktop companion for Linux:</strong> install the <code>.deb</code> or AppImage on x86-64 Linux, connect to a local or remote Gateway, and open Quick Chat from the system tray or an X11 keyboard shortcut. AppImage updates are signature-verified; <code>.deb</code> installs remain under your package manager. See the <a href="https://docs.openclaw.ai/platforms/linux" rel="nofollow">Linux guide</a>.</li>
<li><strong>Start work without switching pages:</strong> create and run a background session from New Session, keep its selected local, cloud, or paired-device placement, and open it from the completion notice. Related <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5224010434" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/128037" data-hovercard-type="issue" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/issues/128037/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/128037">#128037</a>. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5224249414" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/128050" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/128050/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/128050">#128050</a>) Thanks <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/Takhoffman/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/Takhoffman">@Takhoffman</a>.</li>
<li><strong>Safer upgrades:</strong> preserve newer configuration, stop incomplete session migrations before claiming success, and recover a stopped Gateway after a failed update when the installed package or rollback is verified safe. Related <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5044723491" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/118244" data-hovercard-type="issue" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/issues/118244/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/118244">#118244</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="4594319812" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/90551" data-hovercard-type="issue" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/issues/90551/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/90551">#90551</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5301640555" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/134206" data-hovercard-type="issue" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/issues/134206/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/134206">#134206</a>. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5294271547" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/133520" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/133520/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/133520">#133520</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5298236138" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/133979" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/133979/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/133979">#133979</a>) Thanks <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/fuller-stack-dev/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev">@fuller-stack-dev</a>.</li>
<li><strong>More dependable voice:</strong> keep internal reasoning out of speech, preserve tool-generated audio through delivery, and keep later browser Talk turns working after call setup. <h3>Changes</h3>
<ul>
<li>
<p><strong>Recovery cleanup:</strong> preview retained migration originals with <code>openclaw update cleanup --dry-run</code>, then explicitly remove eligible originals while the selected Gateway is stopped; cleanup preserves current SQLite history but permanently gives up rollback to removed originals. Related <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5296598709" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/133805" data-hovercard-type="issue" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/issues/133805/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/133805">#133805</a>. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5297107577" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/133864" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/133864/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/133864">#133864</a>)</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><strong>Session visibility default:</strong> let unsandboxed sessions work with other sessions of the same agent by default, including retained cron sessions; shared-agent operators should set <code>tools.sessions.visibility</code> to <code>tree</code> or <code>self</code> when they need narrower access, while sandbox and cross-agent restrictions remain enforced. Related <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5293746946" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/133456" data-hovercard-type="issue" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/issues/133456/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/133456">#133456</a>. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5293883862" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/133469" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/133469/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/133469">#133469</a>)</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><strong>Cross-session conversations:</strong> render forwarded messages as distinct speech bubbles with source-session links and sending-agent identity, preserving attribution when messages steer an active conversation. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5281628498" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/132054" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/132054/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/132054">#132054</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5293619150" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/133439" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/133439/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/133439">#133439</a>)</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><strong>Session organization:</strong> group session actions into clearer menus, copy transcripts as Markdown, open sessions in tabs, windows, or splits, edit icons and colors together, and optionally hide empty session groups. Related <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5294008675" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/133480" data-hovercard-type="issue" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/issues/133480/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/133480">#133480</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5295036913" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/133629" data-hovercard-type="issue" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/issues/133629/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/133629">#133629</a>. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5294088178" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/133490" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/133490/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/133490">#133490</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5295055313" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/133638" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/133638/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/133638">#133638</a>)</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><strong>Home without switching pages:</strong> dock the selected agent’s existing Home conversation beside your work, retain its draft and attachments when opening it full page, and inspect or remove the “Working on” context before sending. Related <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5295045757" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/133632" data-hovercard-type="issue" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/issues/133632/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/133632">#133632</a>. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5295333543" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/133676" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/133676/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/133676">#133676</a>)</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><strong>Prepared cloud projects:</strong> reuse prepared project snapshots and validated workspace hashes before starting a cloud session, and preserve the required stop, snapshot, and restart cycle for Daytona-backed projects. Related <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5293593120" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/133436" data-hovercard-type="issue" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/issues/133436/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/133436">#133436</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5293686845" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/133450" data-hovercard-type="issue" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/issues/133450/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/133450">#133450</a>. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5293661873" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/133447" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/133447/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/133447">#133447</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5298890662" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/134026" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/134026/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/134026">#134026</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5299087478" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/134043" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/134043/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/134043">#134043</a>)</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><strong>Readable Beam links:</strong> share transcripts through readable <code>/beam/</code> URLs named after their sessions, with existing access checks and contextual navigation preserved. Related <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5180343604" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/125752" data-hovercard-type="issue" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/issues/125752/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/125752">#125752</a>. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5180394255" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/125755" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/125755/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/125755">#125755</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5293798291" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/133463" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/133463/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/133463">#133463</a>)</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><strong>Chrome relay wake-up:</strong> support standalone local relays that start on demand, share their paired browser with the Gateway, and remain available to other CDP clients when the Gateway disconnects; see the <a href="https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/chrome-extension#standalone-direct-loopback-relay" rel="nofollow">Chrome extension guide</a> for supported builds and setup. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5229226742" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/128379" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/128379/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/128379">#128379</a>)</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><strong>Plugin SDK types:</strong> type Telegram <code>botToken</code> as <code>SecretInput</code> (<code>string | SecretRef</code>), matching existing secret-reference support; plugin authors should use the resolved account <code>token</code> and read finalization-context <code>messages</code> only when <code>source === "openclaw-transcript"</code>. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5298371518" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/133988" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/133988/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/133988">#133988</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5302121185" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/134238" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/134238/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/134238">#134238</a>)</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><strong>Background sessions:</strong> start work from New Session without leaving the page with <code>Cmd/Ctrl+Enter</code>, retain the selected local, cloud, or paired-device placement, and open the session from its completion notice; use <code>Cmd/Ctrl+Shift+Enter</code> when Modifier+Enter is already your normal send shortcut, while explicit Draft visibility still creates a draft. Related <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5224010434" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/128037" data-hovercard-type="issue" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/issues/128037/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/128037">#128037</a>. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5224249414" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/128050" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/128050/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/128050">#128050</a>) Thanks <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/Takhoffman/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/Takhoffman">@Takhoffman</a>.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><strong>iOS composer:</strong> bring inline model, thinking, permission, attachment, and context controls closer to the web experience, keeping queued sends bound to the session settings that authorized them. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5287440967" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/132683" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/132683/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/132683">#132683</a>) Thanks <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/Solvely-Colin/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/Solvely-Colin">@Solvely-Colin</a>.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p><strong>Plugin approval verification:</strong> let plugins describe an external verification choice in approval presentations while OpenClaw retains approval identity, authorization, timeouts, and the final decision. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="4974410865" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/113517" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/113517/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/113517">#113517</a>) Thanks <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/Guardiola31337/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/Guardiola31337">@Guardiola31337</a>.</p>
</li>
</ul>
<h3>Fixes</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>Optional image decoding:</strong> update the release’s managed Sharp dependency to 0.35.4 with libheif 1.23.2, fixing vulnerabilities in image decoding while preserving optional installation. See the plugin dependency caveat under Known issues.</li>
<li><strong>Workspace permissions:</strong> apply permission changes to active runs and preserve session tool policies on cloud workers, so changing where work runs does not widen what it may do. <h3>Known issues</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>Optional memory plugin dependency:</strong> standalone <code>@openclaw/memory-lancedb</code> installs can still resolve an older vulnerable Sharp version through an optional Transformers dependency. The plugin’s reviewed text-embedding and vector-storage path does not load that image adapter; this dependency exposure remains a packaging follow-up.</li>
<li><strong>Hindi and Korean token labels:</strong> the native app’s token-usage label reverses the wording for used and total tokens; the counts and calculations are unchanged.</li>
</ul>
<h3>Upcoming deprecations</h3>
<p>These already-deprecated Plugin SDK import paths remain available in <strong>2026.8.2</strong>. Their recorded removal target is <strong>2026-09-01</strong>; plugin authors should prepare the following migrations using the <a href="https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/sdk-migration" rel="nofollow">Plugin SDK migration guide</a>.</p>
<ul>
<li><strong><code>openclaw/plugin-sdk/config-runtime</code>:</strong> use <code>api.pluginConfig</code> for plugin configuration, <code>openclaw/plugin-sdk/config-contracts</code> for types, <code>openclaw/plugin-sdk/runtime-config-snapshot</code> for snapshot reads, and <code>openclaw/plugin-sdk/config-mutation</code> for writes. (<code>plugin-sdk-config-runtime-subpath</code>)</li>
<li><strong><code>openclaw/plugin-sdk/channel-reply-pipeline</code>:</strong> import reply pipeline helpers from <code>openclaw/plugin-sdk/channel-outbound</code>. (<code>plugin-sdk-channel-reply-pipeline-subpath</code>)</li>
<li><strong><code>openclaw/plugin-sdk/infra-runtime</code>:</strong> move helpers to focused imports, including <code>openclaw/plugin-sdk/delivery-queue-runtime</code>, <code>openclaw/plugin-sdk/diagnostic-runtime</code>, <code>openclaw/plugin-sdk/error-runtime</code>, <code>openclaw/plugin-sdk/exec-approvals-runtime</code>, <code>openclaw/plugin-sdk/fetch-runtime</code>, and <code>openclaw/plugin-sdk/ssrf-runtime</code>. The migration guide records that system-event snapshot inspection and consumption still have no modern public replacement. (<code>plugin-sdk-infra-runtime-subpath</code>)</li>
<li><strong><code>openclaw/plugin-sdk/channel-lifecycle</code>:</strong> use <code>openclaw/plugin-sdk/channel-outbound</code>. (<code>plugin-sdk-channel-lifecycle-subpath</code>)</li>
<li><strong><code>openclaw/plugin-sdk/channel-message</code>:</strong> use <code>openclaw/plugin-sdk/channel-outbound</code> and <code>openclaw/plugin-sdk/channel-inbound</code>. (<code>plugin-sdk-channel-message-subpath</code>)</li>
</ul>
